---
title: "Metabase API reference"
sidebarTitle: "API"
description: "API reference for the Metabase plugin in Corsair: every `metabase.api.*` operation with its input and output types, taken from the plugin's Zod schemas."
---

<Info>
**New to Corsair?** See [API access](/concepts/api), [authentication](/concepts/auth), and [error handling](/concepts/error-handling).
</Info>

## Action

### get

`action.get`

Returns the actions the current user can read, unarchived unless `?archived=true`.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.metabase.api.action.get({ "query": {  } });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `query` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="query full type">

```ts
{
  model-id?: number | null,
  type?: implicit | query | null,
  archived?: boolean
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `JsonValue[]`


---

### getByActionId

`action.getByActionId`

Fetch an Action.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.metabase.api.action.getByActionId({ "path": { "action-id": 1 } });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  action-id: number
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `JsonValue`


---

### getPublic

`action.getPublic`

Fetch a list of Actions with public UUIDs.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.metabase.api.action.getPublic({  });
```

**Input:** _empty object_


**Output:** `JsonValue[]`


---

### post

`action.post`

Create a new action.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.metabase.api.action.post({ "body": { "name": "", "type": "implicit" } });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `body` | `JsonValue` | No | — |



**Output:** `JsonValue`


---

### postByActionIdExecuteValues

`action.postByActionIdExecuteValues`

Fetches the values for filling in execution parameters.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.metabase.api.action.postByActionIdExecuteValues({ "path": { "action-id": 1 }, "body": { "parameters": {  } } });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `body` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  action-id: number
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  parameters: Record<string, string | number | boolean | null>
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `Record<string, JsonValue>`


---

### postExecute

`action.postExecute`

Execute the ad-hoc `action`, without saving it.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.metabase.api.action.postExecute({ "body": { "action": { "name": "", "type": "implicit" } } });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `body` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  action: JsonValue,
  parameters?: Record<string, string | number | boolean | null | (string | number | boolean | null)[]> | null
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `Record<string, JsonValue>`

## Agent

### getV1Ping

`agent.getV1Ping`

Health check endpoint for the Agent API.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.metabase.api.agent.getV1Ping({  });
```

**Input:** _empty object_


**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `message` | `string \| null` | No | — |



---

### postV1Collection

`agent.postV1Collection`

Create a new Collection.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.metabase.api.agent.postV1Collection({ "body": { "name": "a" } });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `body` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  description?: string | null,
  name: string,
  parent_collection_id?: number | null
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `description` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `location` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `parent_id` | `number \| null \| null` | No | — |

### postV1ConstructNativeQuery

`agent.postV1ConstructNativeQuery`

Construct a native (raw SQL) query against a database.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.metabase.api.agent.postV1ConstructNativeQuery({ "body": { "database_id": 1, "sql": "a" } });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `body` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  database_id: number,
  sql: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `prompt` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `query` | `string \| null` | No | — |



---

### postV1Dashboard

`agent.postV1Dashboard`

Create a new dashboard, optionally populated with saved questions.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.metabase.api.agent.postV1Dashboard({ "body": { "name": "a" } });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `body` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  collection_id?: number | null,
  description?: string | null,
  name: string,
  question_ids?: number[] | null
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `collection_id` | `number \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `collection_path` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `dashcard_ids` | `number[] \| null` | No | — |
| `description` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `tabs` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `url` | `string \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="tabs full type">

```ts
{
  id?: number | null,
  name?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### postV1Execute

`agent.postV1Execute`

Execute an MBQL query and return results.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.metabase.api.agent.postV1Execute({ "body": { "query": "a" } });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `body` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  query: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `error` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `row_count` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `running_time` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `completed \| failed \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  cols?: {
    base_type?: string | null,
    display_name?: string | null,
    effective_type?: string | null | null,
    name?: string | null
  }[] | null,
  rows?: JsonValue[][] | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

### postV1Metric

`agent.postV1Metric`

Save a previously constructed query as a named metric.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.metabase.api.agent.postV1Metric({ "body": { "name": "a", "query": "a" } });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `body` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  collection_id?: number | null,
  description?: string | null,
  display?: table | bar | line | pie | scatter | area | row | combo | pivot | scalar | smartscalar | gauge | progress | funnel | map | waterfall | sankey | null,
  name: string,
  query: string,
  visualization_settings?: Record<string, JsonValue> | null
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `collection_id` | `number \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `collection_path` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `description` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `display` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `url` | `string \| null` | No | — |



---

`agent.postV1ReadResource`

| Name | Type | Required | Description |

```ts
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `output` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `resources` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |

<Accordion title="resources full type">

```ts
  content?: JsonValue | null,
```

### postV1Search

`agent.postV1Search`

Search for tables, models, metrics, saved questions, dashboards, and collections.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.metabase.api.agent.postV1Search({ "body": {  } });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `body` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  semantic_queries?: string[] | string | null,
  term_queries?: string[] | string | null
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `total_count` | `number \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  collection?: Record<string, JsonValue> | null | null,
  id?: number | null,
  name?: string | null,
  verified?: boolean | null | null,
  location?: string | null | null,
  database_id?: number | null | null,
  updated_at?: string | null | null,
  display_name?: string | null | null,
  type?: table | metric | model | question | dashboard | collection | null,
  official?: boolean | null | null,
  database_schema?: string | null | null,
  created_at?: string | null | null,
  description?: string | null | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### postV2Query

`agent.postV2Query`

Execute a portable MBQL 5 representations JSON query and stream the results, with continuation-token pagination.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.metabase.api.agent.postV2Query({ "body": { "continuation_token": "a" } });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `body` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  continuation_token: string
} | {
  query: string
} | {
  prompt?: string | null,
  query: Record<string, JsonValue>
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `continuation_token` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `error` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `row_count` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `running_time` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `completed \| failed \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  cols?: {
    base_type?: string | null,
    display_name?: string | null,
    effective_type?: string | null | null,
    name?: string | null
  }[] | null,
  rows?: JsonValue[][] | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### putV1DashboardById

`agent.putV1DashboardById`

Update a dashboard.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.metabase.api.agent.putV1DashboardById({ "path": { "id": 1 }, "body": {  } });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `body` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  id: number
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  archived?: boolean | null,
  collection_id?: number | null,
  dashcards?: (
    {
      action: add,
      card_id: number,
      display_size?: wide | tall | full | null,
      tab_id?: number | null
    } | {
      action: add_heading,
      display_size?: null,
      tab_id?: number | null,
      text: string
    } | {
      action: add_text,
      display_size?: wide | tall | full | null,
      tab_id?: number | null,
      text: string
    } | {
      action: update_text,
      dashcard_id: number,
      text: string
    } | {
      action: remove,
      dashcard_id: number
    } | {
      action: move,
      dashcard_id: number,
      position: top | bottom
    }
  )[] | null,
  description?: string | null,
  name?: string | null
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `archived` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `collection_id` | `number \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `collection_path` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `dashcard_ids` | `number[] \| null` | No | — |
| `description` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `tabs` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="tabs full type">

```ts
{
  id?: number | null,
  name?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

## Analytics

### postInternal

`analytics.postInternal`

Receive a batch of internal analytics events from the frontend and record them as Prometheus metrics.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.metabase.api.analytics.postInternal({ "body": { "events": [] } });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `body` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  events: {
    amount?: number | null,
    labels?: Record<string, string> | null,
    metric: string,
    op: inc
  }[]
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `null`


---

## Api Key

### putByIdRegenerate

`apiKey.putByIdRegenerate`

Regenerate an API Key

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.metabase.api.apiKey.putByIdRegenerate({ "path": { "id": 1 } });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |

The OpenAPI pattern `^mb_.*{4}\*+$` is invalid ECMAScript. Metabase's schema description says masked values start with `mb_` and end in asterisks; the plugin validates that documented shape.

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  id: number
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `masked_key` | `string & string \| null` | No | — |
| `prefix` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `unmasked_key` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### graph.update

`collectionPermissions.graph.update`

Replace collection permission graph entries

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.metabase.api.collectionPermissions.graph.update({
	body: {
		groups: {
			2: { 8: 'read' },
		},
		revision: 1,
	},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `query` | `object` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

The permission-graph update can overwrite supplied entries. force=true persists the supplied graph without a revision check and can overwrite concurrent updates; the operation is marked destructive.

```ts
  force?: boolean | null,
  skip-graph?: boolean | null
}
```

```ts
  namespace?: string | null,
  revision?: number | null
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

| Name | Type | Required | Description |

### postByIdPdf

`dashboard.postByIdPdf`

Render Dashboard with ID to a PDF (server-side, the same way dashboard subscriptions render charts) and stream it back as a file download.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.metabase.api.dashboard.postByIdPdf({ "path": { "id": 1 }, "body": {  } });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `body` | `object` | No | — |

The route description specifies a PDF download while the OpenAPI success media/schema is JSON with an unconstrained body. The adapter returns a bounded binary artifact and preserves documented response headers.

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  id: number
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  paper_size?: a4 | letter | null,
  parameters?: JsonValue[] | null
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `kind` | `binary` | Yes | — |
| `contentType` | `string \| null` | Yes | — |
| `base64` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `size` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `headers` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="headers full type">

```ts
{
  contentDisposition?: string,
  contentSecurityPolicy?: string,
  cacheControl?: string,
  etag?: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### postByExportFormat

`dataset.postByExportFormat`

Execute a query and download the result data as a file in the specified format.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.metabase.api.dataset.postByExportFormat({ "path": { "export-format": "csv" }, "body": { "query": { "type": "internal" } } });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `body` | `object` | No | — |

The route description specifies a file download while the OpenAPI success schema references a JSON query result. The adapter follows the explicit file-download description and returns a bounded binary artifact.

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  export-format: csv | api | xlsx | json
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  csv_include_bom?: boolean,
  format_rows?: boolean,
  pivot_results?: boolean,
  query: JsonValue | JsonValue | {
    database: number,
    type: native | query
  },
  visualization_settings?: Record<string, JsonValue>
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `kind` | `binary` | Yes | — |
| `contentType` | `string \| null` | Yes | — |
| `base64` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `size` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `headers` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="headers full type">

```ts
{
  contentDisposition?: string,
  contentSecurityPolicy?: string,
  cacheControl?: string,
  etag?: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### getAppsSandboxHost

`ee.getAppsSandboxHost`

Pro/Enterprise only. Serve the document used as the `src` of the Near-Membrane realm iframe, carrying a per-document CSP that confines `'unsafe-eval'` to that realm.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.metabase.api.ee.getAppsSandboxHost({  });
```

**Input:** _empty object_

The HTML sandbox document is returned as a bounded binary artifact; the Content-Security-Policy header is preserved and the HTML is never executed by the plugin.

| `contentType` | `string \| null` | Yes | — |

| `headers` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="headers full type">

```ts
{
  contentDisposition?: string,
  contentSecurityPolicy?: string,
  cacheControl?: string,
  etag?: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### postCustomVizPlugin

`ee.postCustomVizPlugin`

Pro/Enterprise only. Register a new custom visualization plugin from an uploaded tar.gz bundle.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.metabase.api.ee.postCustomVizPlugin({ "body": { "file": { "filename": "", "size": 20, "tempfile": new Blob(["metabase-test-bundle"]) } } });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `body` | `object` | No | — |

Metabase multipart file input maps to a Corsair Blob-backed file. The default local upload cap is 20 MiB (configurable to 100 MiB); this is a plugin safety cap, not a provider limit.

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  file: {
    filename: string,
    size: number,

  }
```

### getChecklist

### postThreadByThreadIdRestart

### postSourceFeedback

`metabot.postSourceFeedback`

Persist Metabot source feedback.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts

```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `body` | `object` | No | — |

The OpenAPI models a JSON response wrapper with status 204, but HTTP 204 responses have no body. The adapter returns and validates null for the successful empty response.

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  message_id: string,
  metabot_id: number,
  positive: boolean,
  source_id: number,
  source_type: table | card | model
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `null`

### getRuns

`transform.getRuns`

Paginated unified run history: every row is a root run — a job run, a manual DAG-reprocess run, or a standalone transform run — never a member run of a job/DAG (those are listed by `GET /run` and the per-run `transform-runs` endpoints).

### postByIdPasswordResetUrl

`user.postByIdPasswordResetUrl`

Generate a password reset URL for a user.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.metabase.api.user.postByIdPasswordResetUrl({ "path": { "id": 1 } });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  id: number
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `password_reset_url` | `string \| null` | No | — |
